I used to do things. Paint... cook big meals. I used to run before heading to a job site. Even went to theater rehearsals. But... I don't know. Now it all just feels like chores from somebody else's life. I just... I don't care if I miss them anymore.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're moving more slowly than you used to, or have more energy than usual?\n\nPatient: I don't know. I guess I'm just... tired. I used to do things. Paint... cook big meals. I used to run before heading to a job site. Even went to theater rehearsals. But... I don't know. Now it all just feels like chores from somebody else's life. I just... I don't care if I miss them anymore.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're moving more slowly than you used to, or have more energy than usual?\n\nPatient: I don't know. I guess I'm just... tired. I don't really have any energy. I just sit around mostly.\n\nDoctor: You mentioned feeling like dying would be a fair punishment for what you've done to your family and work crew, can you tell me more about what you feel guilty about?\n\nPatient: I just... I ruined things. My husband... he got worse because of me. And the crew... we lost jobs. I messed up the tile work in people's houses. It's like... everything I touch just gets poisoned. So... yeah. I deserve whatever happens.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or seen things that other people couldn't hear or see?\n\nPatient: I... yeah. Sometimes. There's... a man's voice. I hear it when I'm working, or just... in my kitchen. He's always behind me. He says things like... \"You missed it,\" or... \"Everyone knows.\" It's loud. Like he's right there. But... when I turn around, it's just me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're resting or sleeping more than usual?\n\nPatient: I don't know... I guess so. I just lay in bed a lot. I don't really keep track of the time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of time where you felt extremely happy, energetic, or irritable, and you didn't need much sleep?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so. I don't really get energetic like that. Mostly I'm just... tired.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts of suicide or self-harm besides not wanting to wake up?\n\nPatient: I... I don't know about self-harm. But... yeah, I did tell them when I came in that... I keep thinking it would be better if I just didn't wake up. Like... maybe dying would just be... fair. A fair punishment for... what I've done. To my family. And the guys on my crew.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods where these feelings of not wanting to wake up were significantly worse than others?\n\nPatient: I don't know... not really. It's mostly just always there. Since this started, the guilt, and... being so tired. Not caring about anything. Thinking about... not being here. It's just been there most of the time. Everything takes a lot of effort now.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods in the last two weeks where you felt completely unable to get out of bed?\n\nPatient: No... I mean, I get up. It takes a while sometimes, but... I get out of bed.\n\nDoctor: Have you lost a significant amount of weight since you started feeling this way?\n\nPatient: I suppose... yeah. My work pants have gotten loose. I'm not trying to, though. I just... most days I forget to eat until it's really late. And then I'll take a few bites and... I just don't care if I miss them anymore.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt worthless or excessively guilty most of the day, nearly every day?\n\nPatient: Yeah... I guess so. I just... I keep thinking about my husband. How he got worse. I feel like it's my fault. And the crew... they lost work because of me. Clients' houses got messed up. It's like... I don't know. Like I poison everything I touch. I probably deserve whatever happens to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods in the last month where you felt like you were at your lowest point?\n\nPatient: I don't know... I guess maybe some weeks are a little less... crushing. But it doesn't really go away.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any difficulty concentrating or making decisions?\n\nPatient: Yeah... I guess so. It's just... I get so tired. Like, if I just carry one box of tile, or... try to answer a couple of messages from clients... my body just feels heavy. Like wet cement. And my head gets so tired... I just... I can't even make simple decisions after that.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods where you felt completely unable to take care of yourself or perform daily tasks?\n\nPatient: Yeah... The crew, the clients, trying to make the right calls on the job... it wasn't safe anymore. My husband and... my apprentice... they had to start stepping in. Before I ruined a job or... or got someone hurt.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're moving more slowly than you used to?\n\nPatient: I don't know... maybe. I'm just tired. Everything takes a lot of effort now.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods in the last two weeks where you felt completely unable to get out of bed?\n\nPatient: No... I mean, I get up. It takes a while sometimes, but... I get out of bed.\n\nDoctor: Have you lost a significant amount of weight since you started feeling this way?\n\nPatient: I suppose... yeah. My work pants have gotten loose. I'm not trying to, though. I just... most days I forget to eat until it's really late. And then I'll take a few bites and... I just feel sick.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you still enjoy or find pleasure in?\n\nPatient: Not really... no. I used to do things. Paint... cook big meals. I'd run before my install jobs, or go to theater rehearsals. But... I don't know. Now it all just feels like chores from somebody else's life. I just don't care if I miss them anymore.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt worthless or excessively guilty most of the day, nearly every day?\n\nPatient: Yeah... I guess so. I just... I keep thinking about my husband. How he got worse. I feel like it's my fault. And the crew... they lost work because of me. Clients' houses got messed up. It's like... I don't know. Like I poison everything I touch. I probably deserve whatever happens to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods in the last month where you felt like you were at your lowest point?\n\nPatient: I don't know... I guess the whole month has just been a low point. It all just blends together. I don't really keep track of the days anymore.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods in the last two weeks where you felt completely unable to take care of yourself or perform daily tasks without any help?\n\nPatient: No...
